title=\"feat(content): 3-layer fallback guarantees news &amp; media show on Vercel\n\nThe previous static-snapshot fallback in the API route wasn't enough — if the\nAPI route itself fails on Vercel (build error, serverless timeout, Prisma\ninit failure), the frontend had no content at all.\n\nThis adds two more layers of resilience:\n\n1. /public/content.json (committed to repo) — a static JSON file generated\n   from the same seed-data.ts, containing all news, videos, events, leaders,\n   and gallery items. Served as a plain static asset, no serverless function\n   required.\n\n2. src/lib/content-client.ts — a shared fetchContent() helper that tries\n   /api/content first, then falls back to /content.json if the API fails or\n   returns an unexpected shape. Used by all 9 content components:\n   news, events, gallery, nec, faq, home-highlights, policies, video-gallery,\n   about.\n\n3. scripts/generate-static-content.ts — build-time script that regenerates\n   /public/content.json from seed-data.ts. Added to both build and\n   vercel-build scripts with || true so it never fails the build (the\n   committed JSON is always there as a baseline; if bun isn't available on\n   Vercel, the committed version is used as-is).\n\nFallback chain on any deployment:\n  Layer 1: GET /api/content (database-backed)\n  Layer 2: /api/content falls back to in-memory static snapshot (if DB fails)\n  Layer 3: frontend fetches /content.json (if API route fails entirely)\n\nThis guarantees the live site ALWAYS shows all news &amp; media — including the\nPresident Kaalfontein video and July 2026 event posters — regardless of\ndatabase availability, API route health, or deployment environment.\n\nVerified: lint clean, API + static JSON both serve 6 news / 4 videos / 13\nevents / 7 leaders / 16 gallery, home + #news + #gallery all render\ncorrectly in browser.\" class=\"Link--secondary\" href=\"/Lehumo-Tech/The-Arrc/commit/5c2a615cadc6af4775a263fb34a283b83aa937e6\">feat(content): 3-layer fallback guarantees news &amp; media show on Vercel</a></div></div></td><td><div class=\"react-directory-commit-age\"><relative-time data-component=\"RelativeTime\" tense=\"past\" datetime=\"2026-07-12T02:50:49.000Z\" title=\"Jul 12, 2026, 2:50 AM UTC\">Jul 12, 2026</relative-time></div></td></tr><tr class=\"react-directory-row truncate-for-mobile\" id=\"folder-row-62\"><td class=\"react-directory-row-commit-cell\"><div><div class=\"react-directory-commit-message\"><a data-pjax=\"true\" title=\"feat(content): 3-layer fallback guarantees news &amp; media show on Vercel\n\nThe previous static-snapshot fallback in the API route wasn't enough — if the\nAPI route itself fails on Vercel (build error, serverless timeout, Prisma\ninit failure), the frontend had no content at all.\n\nThis adds two more layers of resilience:\n\n1. /public/content.json (committed to repo) — a static JSON file generated\n   from the same seed-data.ts, containing all news, videos, events, leaders,\n   and gallery items. Served as a plain static asset, no serverless function\n   required.\n\n2. src/lib/content-client.ts — a shared fetchContent() helper that tries\n   /api/content first, then falls back to /content.json if the API fails or\n   returns an unexpected shape. Used by all 9 content components:\n   news, events, gallery, nec, faq, home-highlights, policies, video-gallery,\n   about.\n\n3. scripts/generate-static-content.ts — build-time script that regenerates\n   /public/content.json from seed-data.ts. Added to both build and\n   vercel-build scripts with || true so it never fails the build (the\n   committed JSON is always there as a baseline; if bun isn't available on\n   Vercel, the committed version is used as-is).\n\nFallback chain on any deployment:\n  Layer 1: GET /api/content (database-backed)\n  Layer 2: /api/content falls back to in-memory static snapshot (if DB fails)\n  Layer 3: title=\"feat(content): 3-layer fallback guarantees news &amp; media show on Vercel\n\nThe previous static-snapshot fallback in the API route wasn't enough — if the\nAPI route itself fails on Vercel (build error, serverless timeout, Prisma\ninit failure), the frontend had no content at all.\n\nThis adds two more layers of resilience:\n\n1. /public/content.json (committed to repo) — a static JSON file generated\n   from the same seed-data.ts, containing all news, videos, events, leaders,\n   and gallery items. Served as a plain static asset, no serverless function\n   required.\n\n2. src/lib/content-client.ts — a shared fetchContent() helper that tries\n   /api/content first, then falls back to /content.json if the API fails or\n   returns an unexpected shape. Used by all 9 content components:\n   news, events, gallery, nec, faq, home-highlights, policies, video-gallery,\n   about.\n\n3. scripts/generate-static-content.ts — build-time script that regenerates\n   /public/content.json from seed-data.ts. Added to both build and\n   vercel-build scripts with || true so it never fails the build (the\n   committed JSON is always there as a baseline; if bun isn't available on\n   Vercel, the committed version is used as-is).\n\nFallback chain on any deployment:\n  Layer 1: GET /api/content (database-backed)\n  Layer 2: /api/content falls back to in-memory static snapshot (if DB fails)\n  Layer 3: frontend fetches /content.json (if API route fails entirely)\n\nThis guarantees the live site ALWAYS shows all news &amp; media — including the\nPresident Kaalfontein video and July 2026 event posters — regardless of\ndatabase availability, API route health, or deployment environment.\n\nVerified: lint clean, API + static JSON both serve 6 news / 4 videos / 13\nevents / 7 leaders / 16 gallery, home class=\"react-directory-commit-message\"><a data-pjax=\"true\" title=\"feat(content): 3-layer fallback guarantees news &amp; media show on Vercel\n\nThe previous static-snapshot fallback in the API route wasn't enough — if the\nAPI route itself fails on Vercel (build error, serverless timeout, Prisma\ninit failure), the frontend had no content at all.\n\nThis adds two more layers of resilience:\n\n1. /public/content.json (committed to repo) — a static JSON file generated\n   from the same seed-data.ts, containing all news, videos, events, leaders,\n   and gallery items. Served as a plain static asset, no serverless function\n   required.\n\n2. src/lib/content-client.ts — a shared fetchContent() helper that tries\n   /api/content first, then falls back to /content.json if the API fails or\n   returns an unexpected shape. Used by all 9 content components:\n   news, events, gallery, nec, faq, home-highlights, policies, video-gallery,\n   about.\n\n3. scripts/generate-static-content.ts — build-time script that regenerates\n   /public/content.json from seed-data.ts. Added to both build and\n   vercel-build scripts with || true so it never fails the build (the\n   committed JSON is always there as a baseline; if bun isn't available on\n   Vercel, the committed version is used as-is).\n\nFallback chain on any deployment:\n  Layer 1: GET /api/content (database-backed)\n  Layer 2: /api/content falls back to in-memory static snapshot (if DB fails)\n  Layer 3: frontend fetches /content.json (if API route fails entirely)\n\nThis guarantees the live site ALWAYS shows all news &amp; media — including the\nPresident Kaalfontein video and July 2026 event posters — regardless of\ndatabase availability, API route health, or deployment environment.\n\nVerified: lint clean, API + static JSON both serve 6 news / 4 videos / 13\nevents / 7 leaders / 16 gallery, home + #news + #gallery all render\ncorrectly in browser.\" class=\"Link--secondary\" href=\"/Lehumo-Tech/The-Arrc/commit/5c2a615cadc6af4775a263fb34a283b83aa937e6\">feat(content): 3-layer fallback class=\"react-directory-row-commit-cell\"><div><div class=\"react-directory-commit-message\"><a data-pjax=\"true\" title=\"feat(content): 3-layer fallback guarantees news &amp; media show on Vercel\n\nThe previous static-snapshot fallback in the API route wasn't enough — if the\nAPI route itself fails on Vercel (build error, serverless timeout, Prisma\ninit failure), the frontend had no content at all.\n\nThis adds two more layers of resilience:\n\n1. /public/content.json (committed to repo) — a static JSON file generated\n   from the same seed-data.ts, containing all news, videos, events, leaders,\n   and gallery items. Served as a plain static asset, no serverless function\n   required.\n\n2. src/lib/content-client.ts — a shared fetchContent() helper that tries\n   /api/content first, then falls back to /content.json if the API fails or\n   returns an unexpected shape. Used by all 9 content components:\n   news, events, gallery, nec, faq, home-highlights, policies, video-gallery,\n   about.\n\n3. scripts/generate-static-content.ts — build-time script that regenerates\n   /public/content.json from seed-data.ts. Added to both build and\n   vercel-build scripts with || true so it never fails the build (the\n   committed JSON is always there as a baseline; if bun isn't available on\n   Vercel, the committed version is used as-is).\n\nFallback chain on any deployment:\n  Layer 1: GET /api/content (database-backed)\n  Layer 2: /api/content falls back to in-memory static snapshot (if DB fails)\n  Layer 3: frontend fetches /content.json (if API route fails entirely)\n\nThis guarantees the live site ALWAYS shows all news &amp; media — including the\nPresident Kaalfontein video and July 2026 event posters — regardless of\ndatabase availability, API route health, or deployment environment.\n\nVerified: lint clean, API + static JSON both serve 6 news / 4 videos / 13\nevents / 7 leaders / 16 gallery, home + #news + #gallery all render\ncorrectly in browser.\" class=\"Link--secondary\" href=\"/Lehumo-Tech/The-Arrc/commit/5c2a615cadc6af4775a263fb34a283b83aa937e6\">feat(content): 3-layer fallback class=\"react-directory-commit-message\"><a data-pjax=\"true\" title=\"feat(content): 3-layer fallback guarantees news &amp; media show on Vercel\n\nThe previous static-snapshot fallback in the API route wasn't enough — if the\nAPI route itself fails on Vercel (build error, serverless timeout, Prisma\ninit failure), the frontend had no content at all.\n\nThis adds two more layers of resilience:\n\n1. /public/content.json (committed to repo) — a static JSON file generated\n   from the same seed-data.ts, containing all news, videos, events, leaders,\n   and gallery items. Served as a plain static asset, no serverless function\n   required.\n\n2. src/lib/content-client.ts — a shared fetchContent() helper that tries\n   /api/content first, then falls back to /content.json if the API fails or\n   returns an unexpected shape. Used by all 9 content components:\n   news, events, gallery, nec, faq, home-highlights, policies, video-gallery,\n   about.\n\n3. scripts/generate-static-content.ts — build-time script that regenerates\n   /public/content.json from seed-data.ts. Added to both build and\n   vercel-build scripts with || true so it never fails the build (the\n   committed JSON is always there as a baseline; if bun isn't available on\n   Vercel, the committed version is used as-is).\n\nFallback chain on any deployment:\n  Layer 1: GET /api/content (database-backed)\n  Layer 2: /api/content falls back to in-memory static snapshot (if DB fails)\n  Layer 3: frontend fetches /content.json (if API route fails entirely)\n\nThis guarantees the live site ALWAYS shows all news &amp; media — including the\nPresident Kaalfontein video and July 2026 event posters — regardless of\ndatabase availability, API route health, or deployment environment.\n\nVerified: lint clean, API + static JSON both serve 6 news / 4 videos / 13\nevents / 7 leaders / 16 gallery, home + #news + #gallery all render\ncorrectly in browser.\" class=\"Link--secondary\" href=\"/Lehumo-Tech/The-Arrc/commit/5c2a615cadc6af4775a263fb34a283b83aa937e6\">feat(content): 3-layer fallback
